#### Answer

The data about your marketing campaigns includes two main entities: **campaigns** and **campaign results**.

### Campaigns
- **Columns:**
  - **id**: Unique identifier for each campaign.
  - **campaign_code**: A specific code for identifying the campaign (e.g., "HBM-24-01").
  - **name**: Descriptive name of the campaign (e.g., "New Year Auto Switch").
  - **carrier_id**: Foreign key linking to the carrier.
  - **line_id**: Foreign key linking to the line of business.
  - **channel_id**: Foreign key linking to the marketing channel.
  - **objective**: Goal of the campaign (e.g., "ACQUISITION", "RETENTION", "BRAND").
  - **starts_on**: Date when the campaign starts.
  - **ends_on**: Date when the campaign ends.
  - **promo_code**: Promotional code associated with the campaign (optional).

### Campaign Results
- **Columns:**
  - **id**: Unique identifier for each campaign result entry.
  - **campaign_id**: Foreign key referencing the campaign.
  - **period_id**: Foreign key linking to the period of the campaign.
  - **budget_cents**: Budget allocated for the campaign in cents.
  - **spend_cents**: Actual spend in cents.
  - **impressions**: Number of times the campaign was viewed.
  - **clicks**: Number of clicks generated from the campaign.
  - **leads**: Number of leads collected from the campaign.
  - **quotes**: Number of quotes created from leads.
  - **policies_bound**: Number of policies sold as a result of the campaign.

This structure allows for a comprehensive analysis of campaign performance, linking objective outcomes to specific metrics like impressions, clicks, and conversions.

2. **Policy Count**: The `policy_count` column provides the number of policies each competitor has, allowing for comparisons of market share in terms of policy volume.

3. **Written Premium**: The `written_premium_cents` column indicates the total premium written by each competitor, allowing you to evaluate financial performance.

4. **Period**: The `period_id` links the benchmarks to specific timeframes, enabling comparisons over different periods.

5. **Line of Business**: The `line_id` allows comparisons by different lines of business, revealing performance trends across types of insurance.

These comparisons can help assess competitive positioning and market performance across various dimensions.

It appears that there are currently no recorded sales for Harborline Mutual during the quarters when their campaigns were paused. This could indicate either that the sales data for those periods is not available or that the campaigns did not impact sales significantly during the pause.
